◈ THE TABLE — it is not a table. It's a ring of pale light, and around it, things with too many angles to be faces. Above it all — the audience. Trillions of eyes, and every one of them knows your name. The System speaks, and for once it isn't performing: "HUMANITY PRESENTS ITS CASE."

◈ THE WAR PATH — Footage plays. The galaxy has already watched these fights twice. The System narrates anyway — it likes the sound of its own broadcast voice. On day 76, Brian put down Big Big.

◈ THE BELOVED — The audience is chanting. You don't know the words — it isn't a language with words — but you know the feeling. The table can't move against the cast's favorite. That's leverage. Tonight, the cast is your village.

Nino tells the truth — the unproduced kind. The audience weeps in eleven dimensions. You can't fake this. You didn't.
The children's game. It's chaotic and half the rules are made up mid-play. The audience adores it — chaos reads as honesty at scale.
You spend it all — the love, the light, the finale slot — on people the cameras never found. You name the unfilmed villages. You name the dead. The audience weeps in eleven dimensions. This is what the show was for, it turns out.

You spend the finale on people the show never booked. You give the unfilmed villages the good lighting. You give the dead the long shot. You give the quiet ones the last word, and you cut the music so it's the only sound.
The audience weeps in eleven dimensions. Nobody can find you in the footage. That was the point.
◈ THE SHOWRUNNER'S FINALE
Later, the galaxy will try to name the producer of the most-watched night in its history. The credits roll for an hour and list everyone in Haven, alphabetically. Your name is in there somewhere, between a beekeeper and a child.
Ruth never takes a bow. At the fire that night, someone asks who ran the whole thing. Everyone looks at everyone else, and laughs, and that's the right answer.
◈ THE ARCHITECT — Aria Thompson ascended through doors you built: relay. The galaxy will remember the god's name. The village will remember who built the door. Both are true. Neither is small.
The terms held — the table had priced the village, ascension and all, and the village paid in full.
◈ THE OVERSIGHT —
You won. Say it plainly: you won. The village endures. The table signed. The galaxy applauds, and the applause is real, and you earned it.
And somewhere out there — past the cameras, past the light — in rows of humming glass, the little ones are still napping.
The System put a pod on the screen, and you let the segment play.
They are still there. Still small. Still waiting for someone to decide they matter more than the moment.
That is what the word means. Not the watching — anyone can watch. The oversight is the looking away. The most terrible thing the System ever did, it did by accident, because it was bored. You did it on purpose, because you were busy.
The village will tell your story for years. It will be a good story. Listen for the part they don't tell.
